   457→### Task 6: Architecture Documentation
   458→
   459→**Files:**
   460→- Create: `ARCHITECTURE.md`
   461→- Modify: `custom_components/lock_code_manager/coordinator.py:1`
   462→- Modify: `custom_components/lock_code_manager/binary_sensor.py:1`
   463→- Modify: `custom_components/lock_code_manager/providers/zwave_js.py:1`
   464→- Modify: `custom_components/lock_code_manager/providers/_base.py:1`
   465→
   466→**Step 1: Create ARCHITECTURE.md**
   467→
   468→Write `ARCHITECTURE.md` covering:
   469→
   470→1. **Overview** — LCM manages usercodes across locks via providers. Each lock gets a coordinator that holds the full slot→code mapping (managed AND unmanaged).
   471→
   472→2. **Data Flow** diagram (text):
   473→   ```
   474→   Config Entry (desired state)
   475→       ↓
   476→   Binary Sensor (sync decision)
   477→       ↓ set/clear
   478→   Provider (async_set_usercode / async_clear_usercode)
   479→       ↓ Z-Wave service call
   480→   Lock (firmware)
   481→       ↓ push notification / poll response
   482→   Coordinator (actual state: ALL slots)
   483→       ↓ listener notification
   484→   Binary Sensor (re-evaluate sync)
   485→   ```
   486→
   487→3. **Coordinator** — Stores `dict[int, int | str]` mapping slot number to code for ALL slots on the lock. Does not distinguish managed vs unmanaged. Push-based providers set `update_interval = None`; poll-based providers use periodic refresh.
   488→
   489→4. **Push vs Poll vs Hard Refresh**:
   490→   - **Push**: Z-Wave value update events → provider filters → `coordinator.push_update()`
   491→   - **Poll**: Coordinator's `_async_update_data()` → provider's `async_get_usercodes()` on interval
   492→   - **Hard refresh**: `async_hard_refresh_codes()` → refreshes Z-Wave CC values cache from device, then reads all slots
   493→
   494→5. **Managed vs Unmanaged Slots** — A slot is "managed" if it exists in any LCM config entry's `CONF_SLOTS` for this lock AND the corresponding LCM entities exist. Detection: `_get_slot_entity_states()` returns `None` for unmanaged. Coordinator stores both; sync only operates on managed; UI displays both.
   495→
   496→6. **Sync Decision** — Binary sensor compares desired state (PIN from text entity, active from condition) against actual state (coordinator data). If active + PIN differs → set. If inactive + code present → clear.
   497→
   498→7. **Masked PINs** — Some locks return `****` instead of actual codes. For managed slots, resolved via the text entity's expected PIN. For unmanaged slots, kept as-is (just needs non-empty to indicate "in use").
   499→
   500→8. **Duplicate Code Detection** — Pre-flight check in `async_set_usercode()` scans coordinator data. Event 15 handler as safety net for masked/unknown slots.
   501→
   502→9. **Rate Limiting** — All lock operations serialized via `asyncio.Lock` per provider instance, with 2-second minimum delay between operations.
   503→
   504→**Step 2: Update module docstrings**
   505→
   506→Each module gets 2-3 sentences + pointer to `ARCHITECTURE.md`:
   507→
   508→- `coordinator.py`: "Manages the slot→code mapping for a single lock. Stores ALL slots (managed and unmanaged). See ARCHITECTURE.md for the full data flow."
   509→- `binary_sensor.py`: "Sync sensor that compares desired state (config entries) against actual state (coordinator data) and triggers set/clear operations to reconcile. See ARCHITECTURE.md for the sync decision flow."
   510→- `providers/zwave_js.py`: "Z-Wave JS lock provider. Handles push updates, masked PIN resolution, duplicate code detection, and rate-limited set/clear operations. See ARCHITECTURE.md for the provider's role in the data flow."
   511→- `providers/_base.py`: "Abstract base for lock providers. Handles rate limiting, connection checking, and coordinator refresh after operations. See ARCHITECTURE.md for the provider interface contract."
   512→
   513→**Step 3: Run pre-commit**
   514→

   516→Expected: PASS

   522→```

  "content": "# Plan: Duplicate Code Detection + Architecture Documentation\n\n## Context\n\nUsers report infinite sync loops when a lock rejects a PIN that duplicates another slot's code. PR #899 added reactive handling via Z-Wave event 15, but some locks don't emit this event reliably. Since the coordinator already stores ALL slots (managed + unmanaged), we can detect duplicates pre-flight before sending to the lock.\n\nAdditionally, the codebase lacks documentation on the data flow between coordinator, provider, and sync sensor, and the managed/unmanaged distinction is implicit.\n\n## Tasks\n\n### Task 1: Add DuplicateCodeError Exception\n- **Modify:** `custom_components/lock_code_manager/exceptions.py` — add `DuplicateCodeError` with `code_slot`, `conflicting_slot`, `conflicting_slot_managed`, `lock_entity_id` attributes\n- **Test:** `tests/test_exceptions.py` — verify attributes and message formatting for managed/unmanaged\n\n### Task 2: Pre-flight Duplicate Check in async_set_usercode\n- **Modify:** `custom_components/lock_code_manager/providers/zwave_js.py:580-628` — after the \"already set\" check, scan `self.coordinator.data` for matching PINs. Skip masked values (`****`), skip target slot. Raise `DuplicateCodeError` with managed/unmanaged info via `_get_slot_entity_states()`.\n- **Test:** `tests/providers/test_zwave_js.py` — duplicate found raises, masked slots skipped, no coordinator data proceeds normally\n\n### Task 3: Handle DuplicateCodeError in Sync Path\n- **Modify:** `custom_components/lock_code_manager/binary_sensor.py:502-542` — catch `DuplicateCodeError` in `_perform_sync_operation`, disable slot via switch service, create persistent notification with conflicting slot details, cancel retry (permanent error)\n- **Test:** `tests/test_binary_sensor.py` — verify slot disabled, notification created, no retry scheduled\n\n### Task 4: Verify DuplicateCodeError Propagates on Direct Set\n- **Verify:** `websocket.py` `set_lock_usercode` — `DuplicateCodeError` extends `HomeAssistantError`, should propagate naturally. Write test confirming error response.\n- **Test:** `tests/test_websocket.py`\n\n### Task 5: Config Flow Text Updates\n- **Modify:** `custom_components/lock_code_manager/strings.json` + `translations/en.json`\n  - `choose_path.description`: Add guidance about single-tool management, clearing slots before setup, not setting codes manually\n  - `ui.description`: \"Configure slots for all codes you want on the lock (existing user codes for the slot range specified will be cleared).\"\n\n### Task 6: Architecture Documentation\n- **Create:** `ARCHITECTURE.md` — data flow diagram, coordinator model, push/poll/refresh, managed vs unmanaged, sync decision, masked PINs, duplicate detection, rate limiting\n- **Modify:** Module docstrings in `coordinator.py`, `binary_sensor.py`, `zwave_js.py`, `_base.py` — 2-3 sentences + pointer to ARCHITECTURE.md\n\n### Task 7: Verify TODOs\n- Confirm TODO.md has the 5 future items: config flow reset step, conflicting integration detection, update migration docs, clear all unmanaged UI action, lock-specific carve-outs\n\n### Task 8: Final Verification\n- Full test suite, pre-commit, PR\n\n## Key Design Decisions\n\n- **DuplicateCodeError (exception B approach):** Provider raises, callers decide policy. Sync path disables + notifies. Direct path propagates error.\n- **Masked slots:** Can't detect duplicates against `****` values. Event 15 handler stays as safety net.\n- **Architecture doc over module docstrings:** Cross-cutting data flow belongs in one place, not split across 4 files.\n\n## Verification\n\n1. `pytest` — all tests pass\n2. `pre-commit run --all-files` — passes\n3. Manually verify: set duplicate PIN → sync path disables slot + notification; direct websocket set → error response\n"
}
